     3	Runs every 30 s during deploy and for 1 h post-deploy, detects three

    96	def load_slo_config(path: Path | str = DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH) -> SloConfig:
    97	    raw = yaml.safe_load(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    98	    routes = {
    99	        route: RouteSlo(
   100	            error_rate_lt=float(data["error_rate_lt"]),
   101	            p95_latency_ms_lt=float(data["p95_latency_ms_lt"]),
   102	            success_rate_gt=float(data["success_rate_gt"]),
   103	        )
   104	        for route, data in raw["routes"].items()
   105	    }
   106	    if "*" not in routes:
   107	        raise ValueError("config/slos.yaml must define routes['*']")
   108	    return SloConfig(
   109	        interval_seconds=int(raw["interval_seconds"]),
   110	        post_deploy_monitor_seconds=int(raw["post_deploy_monitor_seconds"]),
   111	        breach_consecutive_windows=int(raw["breach_consecutive_windows"]),
   112	        rollback_max_seconds=int(raw["rollback_max_seconds"]),
   113	        error_budget_seconds=int(raw["error_budget"]["seconds"]),
   114	        routes=routes,
   115	    )

   117	
   118	class PrometheusMetricSource:
   119	    """Minimal Prometheus HTTP API client for route-level SLO windows."""
   120	
   121	    def __init__(self, base_url: str, *, routes: Sequence[str] = ("*",)) -> None:
   122	        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
   123	        self.routes = tuple(routes) or ("*",)
   124	
   125	    def fetch(self, window_seconds: int) -> Sequence[RouteWindow]:
   126	        return [self._fetch_route(route, window_seconds) for route in self.routes]
   127	
   128	    def _fetch_route(self, route: str, window_seconds: int) -> RouteWindow:
   129	        selector = "" if route == "*" else f',route="{route}"'
   130	        span = f"{window_seconds}s"
   131	        total = f'sum(rate(http_requests_total{{{selector.lstrip(",")}}}[{span}]))'
   132	        errors = f'sum(rate(http_requests_total{{status=~"5.."{selector}}}[{span}]))'
   133	        success = f'sum(rate(http_requests_total{{status!~"5.."{selector}}}[{span}]))'
   134	        latency = (
   135	            "histogram_quantile(0.95, "
   136	            f"sum(rate(http_request_duration_seconds_bucket{{{selector.lstrip(',')}}}[{span}])) by (le))"
   137	            ") * 1000"
   138	        )
   139	        total_value = self._query(total)
   140	        if total_value <= 0:
   141	            return RouteWindow(route=route, error_rate=0.0, p95_latency_ms=0.0, success_rate=1.0)
   142	        return RouteWindow(
   143	            route=route,
   144	            error_rate=self._query(errors) / total_value,
   145	            p95_latency_ms=self._query(latency),
   146	            success_rate=self._query(success) / total_value,
   147	        )
   148	
   149	    def _query(self, promql: str) -> float:
   150	        url = self.base_url + "/api/v1/query?" + urllib.parse.urlencode({"query": promql})
   151	        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=10) as resp:
   152	            payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
   153	        results = payload.get("data", {}).get("result", [])
   154	        if not results:
   155	            return 0.0
   156	        return float(results[0]["value"][1])
   157	
   158	
   159	class ConsecutiveBreachDetector:
   160	    def __init__(self, config: SloConfig) -> None:
   161	        self.config = config
   162	        self._counts: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {}
   163	
   164	    def observe(self, windows: Sequence[RouteWindow]) -> list[SloBreach]:
   165	        breaches: list[SloBreach] = []
   166	        seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
   167	        for window in windows:
   168	            slo = self.config.route_slo(window.route)
   169	            checks = (
   170	                ("error_rate", window.error_rate, slo.error_rate_lt, window.error_rate >= slo.error_rate_lt),
   171	                (
   172	                    "p95_latency_ms",
   173	                    window.p95_latency_ms,
   174	                    slo.p95_latency_ms_lt,
   175	                    window.p95_latency_ms >= slo.p95_latency_ms_lt,
   176	                ),
   177	                (
   178	                    "success_rate",
   179	                    window.success_rate,
   180	                    slo.success_rate_gt,
   181	                    window.success_rate <= slo.success_rate_gt,
   182	                ),
   183	            )
   184	            for metric, observed, threshold, failed in checks:
   185	                key = (window.route, metric)
   186	                seen.add(key)
   187	                self._counts[key] = self._counts.get(key, 0) + 1 if failed else 0
   188	                if self._counts[key] == self.config.breach_consecutive_windows:
   189	                    breaches.append(
   190	                        SloBreach(
   191	                            route=window.route,
   192	                            metric=metric,
   193	                            observed=observed,
   194	                            threshold=threshold,
   195	                            windows=self._counts[key],
   196	                        )
   197	                    )
   198	        for key in set(self._counts) - seen:
   199	            self._counts[key] = 0
   200	        return breaches
   201

   211	        cwd=str(PROJECT_ROOT),
   212	        env=dict(env) if env is not None else None,
   213	        timeout=timeout,
   214	        capture_output=True,
   215	        text=True,
   216	        check=False,
   217	    )

   224	    source = env if env is not None else os.environ

   240	        f"{registry}/frontend:{tag}",

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""OP-883 D11 -- continuous SLO monitor with auto-rollback on breach.
     2	
     3	This is the **orchestrator-side** SLO monitor sitting one level above
     4	:mod:`backend.slo_monitor` (the OP-772 deploy-window worker that wraps
     5	Prometheus + ``docker compose``). The D11 monitor runs continuously in
     6	production and decides between **D10 canary** rollback and **D9 full**
     7	prod rollback when an SLO breach is sustained past the AC #3 window.
     8	
     9	The five acceptance criteria from META OP-761 §Phase 3:
    10	
    11	* **AC #1** — :meth:`SloMonitor.tick` samples the metric source every
    12	  ``sample_interval_seconds`` (default 30s, set by
    13	  ``config/slo_thresholds.yaml``). The daemon loop in
    14	  :func:`run_forever` is a thin sleep-around-``tick`` wrapper so tests
    15	  can drive ticks deterministically without touching wall time.
    16	* **AC #2** — two SLOs are evaluated independently: error rate < 1%
    17	  (1-min window) AND p95 latency < 500ms (5-min window). The
    18	  :class:`MetricSource` returns both numbers per tick; the windows are
    19	  the responsibility of the source (Prometheus ``rate``/``histogram_
    20	  quantile`` queries take ``[1m]`` / ``[5m]`` respectively).
    21	* **AC #3** — once a sustained breach (>2min, i.e. ``ceil(120/30)+1=5``
    22	  consecutive sampling ticks under the default cadence) is detected the
    23	  monitor emits ``slo.breach`` on the global event bus AND invokes the
    24	  injected :class:`RollbackTrigger`. The trigger's :attr:`mode` field
    25	  decides whether D10 canary abort or D9 full prod rollback fires --
    26	  the production wiring (:func:`build_default_rollback`) picks canary
    27	  whenever a ``canary_state.json`` exists and the rollout is still
    28	  ``running``/``paused``; otherwise it falls back to the D9 full path.
    29	* **AC #4** — after a rollback fires the monitor sets a 10-min
    30	  cooldown gate. Any tick whose ``now < cooldown_until`` returns
    31	  :attr:`TickAction.cooldown_skip` (logs and emits no SSE event so the
    32	  dashboard isn't spammed mid-incident).
    33	* **AC #5** — the operator override flag ``slo:monitor:suppress`` (a
    34	  file in :data:`DEFAULT_SUPPRESS_FLAG_DIR` *or* the
    35	  ``OMNISIGHT_SLO_MONITOR_SUPPRESS`` env var) short-circuits the
    36	  evaluation. Suppress is intended for known transient incidents
    37	  (planned partner outage etc.) and reads-fresh every tick so an
    38	  operator can toggle it without restarting the daemon.
    39	
    40	Error catalog (matches the ticket description verbatim):
    41	
    42	* :class:`SLOBreachDetected` -- raised internally when sustained breach
    43	  fires; surfaces to the operator notify + SSE event so the on-call
    44	  page goes out alongside the auto-rollback.
    45	* :class:`MetricSourceUnavailable` -- raised by :class:`MetricSource`
    46	  when Prometheus is unreachable. **Fail-open**: the monitor logs a
    47	  warning and returns :attr:`TickAction.metric_unavailable` -- we
    48	  refuse to auto-rollback when our own measurement plane goes dark
    49	  (a Prometheus outage is not evidence that the prod stack is bad).
    50	* :class:`CooldownInEffect` -- raised when an operator manually
    51	  invokes :meth:`SloMonitor.maybe_rollback` from the runbook while the
    52	  cooldown window is still open; the loop path never sees this because
    53	  it checks the gate before dispatching.
    54	
    55	Wiring notes
    56	------------
    57	:class:`SloMonitor` accepts the metric source / override / trigger as
    58	constructor injections so the test suite can pin the sequence without
    59	touching Prometheus, the file system, or Docker. Production wiring
    60	lives in :func:`build_default_monitor` and is intentionally lazy: the
    61	expensive imports (Prometheus client, canary_rollout) only fire when
    62	the daemon entry point asks for them.
    63	"""
    64	from __future__ import annotations
    65	
    66	import logging
    67	import os
    68	import time
    69	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    70	from pathlib import Path
    71	from typing import Any, Callable, Protocol
    72	
    73	import yaml
    74	
    75	from backend import events
    76	
    77	
    78	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    79	
    80	
    81	PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    82	DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = PROJECT_ROOT / "config" / "slo_thresholds.yaml"
    83	DEFAULT_SUPPRESS_FLAG_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "deploy" / "blue-green"
    84	DEFAULT_SUPPRESS_FLAG_NAME = "slo_monitor_suppress.flag"

   200	    def fetch(

   202	        *,
   203	        error_rate_window_seconds: int,
   204	        p95_window_seconds: int,
   205	    ) -> SloSample:
   206	        """Return the current SLO sample.
   207	
   208	        Raises :class:`MetricSourceUnavailable` when the backend is
   209	        unreachable so the monitor can fail-open.
   210	        """
   211	
   212	
   213	class OverrideFlagSource(Protocol):
   214	    def is_suppressed(self) -> bool:
   215	        """True when the operator has set the ``slo:monitor:suppress`` flag."""
   216	
   217	
   218	class RollbackTrigger(Protocol):
   219	    @property
   220	    def mode(self) -> str:
   300	        default_factory=lambda: DEFAULT_SUPPRESS_FLAG_DIR
   301	        / DEFAULT_SUPPRESS_FLAG_NAME
   302	    )
   303	    env_var: str = SUPPRESS_ENV_VAR
   304	
   305	    def is_suppressed(self) -> bool:
   306	        if os.environ.get(self.env_var, "").strip().lower() in {
   307	            "1", "true", "yes", "on",
   308	        }:
   309	            return True
   310	        try:
   311	            return self.flag_path.is_file()
   312	        except OSError:
   313	            return False
   314	
   315	
   316	# ─── SloMonitor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   317	
   318	
   319	@dataclass
   320	class SloMonitor:
   321	    """Continuous monitor + auto-rollback decision engine.
   322	
   323	    The monitor is single-tenant and not thread-safe; production runs
   324	    one instance per worker. Use :meth:`tick` from a scheduler or
   325	    :meth:`run_forever` for a self-pacing daemon loop. ``clock`` and
   326	    ``sleeper`` are injectable so tests can advance time deterministically.
   327	    """
   328	
   329	    thresholds: SloThresholds
   330	    source: MetricSource
   331	    override_source: OverrideFlagSource
   332	    rollback: RollbackTrigger
   333	    clock: Callable[[], float] = field(default=time.monotonic)
   334	    sleeper: Callable[[float], Any] = field(default=time.sleep)
   335	    notify: Callable[[str, str], None] | None = None
   336	
   337	    _first_breach_at: float | None = field(default=None, init=False, repr=False)
   338	    _cooldown_until: float = field(default=0.0, init=False, repr=False)
   339	
   340	    # ── public ──
   341	
   342	    def tick(self) -> TickResult:
   343	        """Run one evaluation cycle. Returns the decision taken so a
   344	        scheduler can log / surface it."""
   345	        now = self.clock()
   346	
   347	        # AC #4: cooldown gate -- short-circuit BEFORE touching the
   348	        # override or the metric source so the post-rollback recovery
   349	        # window is silent on the dashboard.
   350	        if now < self._cooldown_until:
   351	            remaining = self._cooldown_until - now
   352	            return TickResult(
   353	                action=TickAction.cooldown_skip,
   354	                detail=f"cooldown_remaining_s={remaining:.0f}",
   355	            )
   356	
   357	        # AC #5: operator override -- evaluated fresh every tick.
   358	        if self.override_source.is_suppressed():
   359	            # Suppression also clears any in-flight breach streak so
   360	            # the monitor doesn't fire the second the operator removes
   361	            # the flag mid-incident (that would defeat the point of
   362	            # the override).
   363	            self._first_breach_at = None
   364	            return TickResult(action=TickAction.suppressed)
   365	
   366	        # AC #1: pull a sample.
   367	        try:
   368	            sample = self.source.fetch(
   369	                error_rate_window_seconds=self.thresholds.error_rate_window_seconds,
   370	                p95_window_seconds=self.thresholds.p95_window_seconds,
   371	            )
   372	        except MetricSourceUnavailable as exc:
   373	            # Error catalog: fail-open + log warning. We also reset the
   374	            # breach streak: we cannot confirm the breach is still
   375	            # happening if our measurement plane is dark, and we must
   376	            # not let "monitor outage" graduate into "auto-rollback".
   377	            logger.warning("SLO monitor metric source unavailable: %s", exc)
   378	            self._first_breach_at = None
   379	            return TickResult(
   380	                action=TickAction.metric_unavailable, detail=str(exc),
   381	            )
   382	
   383	        # AC #2: evaluate each SLO independently.
   384	        breached = self._breached_metrics(sample)
   385	        axis_status = self._axis_status(sample)
   386	        self._publish_status(sample=sample, axis_status=axis_status)
   387	
   388	        if not breached:
   389	            # Healthy tick -- clear any in-flight streak so a transient
   390	            # spike doesn't accumulate across recoveries.
   391	            self._first_breach_at = None
   392	            return TickResult(
   393	                action=TickAction.ok,
   394	                sample=sample,
   395	                axis_status=axis_status,
   396	            )
   397	
   398	        # AC #3: track sustain window. The streak starts at the FIRST
   399	        # breaching sample, and we trigger only once ``now -
   400	        # first_breach_at`` reaches ``breach_sustain_seconds``.
   401	        if self._first_breach_at is None:
   402	            self._first_breach_at = now
   403	
   404	        elapsed_breach = now - self._first_breach_at
   405	        if elapsed_breach < self.thresholds.breach_sustain_seconds:
   406	            return TickResult(
   407	                action=TickAction.breach_pending,
   408	                sample=sample,
   409	                breached_metrics=tuple(breached),
   410	                axis_status=axis_status,
   411	                detail=(
   412	                    f"breach_for_s={elapsed_breach:.0f} "
   413	                    f"sustain_threshold_s={self.thresholds.breach_sustain_seconds}"
   414	                ),
   415	            )
   416	
   417	        # Sustained breach -- fire SSE + invoke the trigger.
   418	        outcome = self._fire_rollback(sample=sample, breached=tuple(breached))
   419	        # AC #4: arm cooldown regardless of trigger outcome -- the
   420	        # only safe move after an auto-rollback attempt is to wait.
   421	        self._cooldown_until = now + self.thresholds.cooldown_seconds
   422	        self._first_breach_at = None
   423	        return TickResult(
   424	            action=TickAction.rollback_triggered,
   425	            sample=sample,
   426	            breached_metrics=tuple(breached),
   427	            rollback=outcome,
   428	            axis_status=axis_status,
   429	        )
   430	
   560	
   561	    def __init__(self, base_url: str) -> None:
   562	        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
   563	
   564	    def fetch(
   565	        self,
   566	        *,
   567	        error_rate_window_seconds: int,
   568	        p95_window_seconds: int,
   569	    ) -> SloSample:
   570	        import json
   571	        import urllib.error
   572	        import urllib.parse
   573	        import urllib.request
   574	
   575	        def _query(promql: str) -> float:
   576	            url = (
   577	                self.base_url
   578	                + "/api/v1/query?"
   579	                + urllib.parse.urlencode({"query": promql})
   580	            )
   581	            try:
   582	                with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=10) as resp:
   583	                    payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
   584	            except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:
   585	                raise MetricSourceUnavailable(
   586	                    f"Prometheus query failed: {exc}"
   587	                ) from exc
   588	            results = payload.get("data", {}).get("result", [])
   589	            if not results:
   590	                return 0.0
   591	            return float(results[0]["value"][1])
   592	
   593	        err_span = f"{error_rate_window_seconds}s"
   594	        lat_span = f"{p95_window_seconds}s"
   595	        total = f"sum(rate(http_requests_total[{err_span}]))"
   596	        errors = (
   597	            'sum(rate(http_requests_total{status=~"5.."}'
   598	            f"[{err_span}]))"
   599	        )
   600	        latency = (
   601	            "histogram_quantile(0.95, "
   602	            "sum(rate(http_request_duration_seconds_bucket"
   603	            f"[{lat_span}])) by (le)) * 1000"
   604	        )
   605	        project_state_api_p95 = "project_state_api_p95_ms"
   606	        cognee_query_p95 = "cognee_query_p95_ms"
   607	        graphiti_query_p95 = "graphiti_query_p95_ms"
   608	        failure_recall_p95 = "failure_recall_p95_ms"
   609	        total_value = _query(total)
   610	        error_rate = 0.0 if total_value <= 0 else _query(errors) / total_value
   611	        return SloSample(
   612	            error_rate=error_rate,
   613	            p95_latency_ms=_query(latency),
   614	            observed_at=time.time(),
   615	            project_state_api_p95_ms=_query(project_state_api_p95),
   616	            cognee_query_p95_ms=_query(cognee_query_p95),
   617	            graphiti_query_p95_ms=_query(graphiti_query_p95),
   618	            failure_recall_p95_ms=_query(failure_recall_p95),
   619	        )
   620	
   621	
   622	@dataclass
   623	class _CompositeRollbackTrigger:
   624	    """Picks D10 canary abort when a canary rollout is active, else D9
   625	    full prod rollback. The two delegates are injected so the
   626	    integration plumbing can be tested without Docker/Caddy."""
   627	
   628	    canary_trigger: Callable[[str], RollbackOutcome]
   629	    full_trigger: Callable[[str], RollbackOutcome]
   630	    canary_active: Callable[[], bool]
   631	
   632	    @property
   633	    def mode(self) -> str:
   634	        return "canary" if self.canary_active() else "full"
   635	
   636	    def trigger(self, reason: str) -> RollbackOutcome:
   637	        if self.canary_active():
   638	            return self.canary_trigger(reason)
   639	        return self.full_trigger(reason)
   640	
   700	    prom_url = os.environ.get(
   701	        "OMNISIGHT_PROMETHEUS_URL", "http://localhost:9090",
   702	    )
   703	
   704	    def _notify(title: str, message: str) -> None:
   705	        # Lazy import: keep notifications optional in headless contexts.
   706	        try:
   707	            import asyncio
   708	
   709	            from backend.models import NotificationLevel, Severity
   710	            from backend.notifications import notify
   711	
   712	            async def _send() -> None:
   713	                await notify(
   714	                    NotificationLevel.critical,
   715	                    title,
   716	                    message,
   717	                    source="slo-monitor",
   718	                    severity=Severity.P1,
   719	                )
   720	
   721	            try:
   722	                loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   723	            except RuntimeError:
   724	                asyncio.run(_send())
   725	            else:
   726	                loop.create_task(_send())
   727	        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover -- notify must not block
   728	            logger.exception("SLO monitor notify dispatch failed")
   729	
   730	    return SloMonitor(
   731	        thresholds=thresholds,
   732	        source=_PrometheusMetricSource(prom_url),
   733	        override_source=FileOrEnvOverrideSource(),
   734	        rollback=build_default_rollback(),
   735	        notify=_notify,
   736	    )
   737	
   738	
   739	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:  # pragma: no cover -- daemon
   740	    import argparse
   741	
   742	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="OP-883 D11 SLO monitor")
   743	    parser.add_argument("--config", default=str(DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH))
   744	    parser.add_argument(
   745	        "--max-iterations", type=int, default=None,
   746	        help="Stop after N ticks (for soak tests). Default: run forever.",
   747	    )
   748	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   749	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
   750	    monitor = build_default_monitor(config_path=args.config)
   751	    monitor.run_forever(max_iterations=args.max_iterations)
   752	    return 0
   753	
   754	
   755	if __name__ == "__main__":  # pragma: no cover
   756	    raise SystemExit(main())

    46	async def _startup_cleanup(log):
    47	    """Reset stuck states left over from a previous crash."""
    48	    # 1. Reset agents stuck in "running" for over 1 hour.
    49	    # created_at is stored as TEXT (ISO-8601-like "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS")
    50	    # in both SQLite and PG. Phase-3 cutover: rather than teaching the
    51	    # pg_compat shim to rewrite SQLite's 2-arg ``datetime('now','-1 hour')``
    52	    # + 1-arg ``datetime(col)`` forms (which are harder to disambiguate
    53	    # from column identifiers in a regex), we compute the cutoff string
    54	    # in Python and do a plain text comparison — deterministic for our
    55	    # insertion format and dialect-neutral.
    56	    from datetime import datetime as _dt, timedelta as _td
    57	    _cutoff = (_dt.utcnow() - _td(hours=1)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    58	    n = await db.execute_raw(
    59	        "UPDATE agents SET status='idle', thought_chain='[RECOVERY] Reset on startup' "
    60	        "WHERE status='running' AND created_at < ?",
    61	        (_cutoff,),
    62	    )
    63	    if n:
    64	        log.warning("Startup cleanup: reset %d stuck agents to idle", n)
    65	    # 2. Reset simulations stuck in "running"
    66	    n = await db.execute_raw(
    67	        "UPDATE simulations SET status='error' WHERE status='running'"
    68	    )
    69	    if n:
    70	        log.warning("Startup cleanup: marked %d stuck simulations as error", n)
    71	    # 3. Clean orphaned Docker containers
    72	    try:
    73	        from backend.container import cleanup_orphaned_containers
    74	        removed = await cleanup_orphaned_containers()
    75	        if removed:
    76	            log.warning("Startup cleanup: removed %d orphaned containers", removed)
    77	    except Exception:
    78	        pass  # Docker may not be available
    79	    # 4. Clean stale git lock files
    80	    try:
    81	        from backend.workspace import cleanup_stale_locks
    82	        await cleanup_stale_locks()
    83	    except Exception:
    84	        pass
    85	    # 5. R8 #314 row 2875: scan + remove orphan worktrees left over
    86	    # from a prior crashed/restarted process. ``_workspaces`` is empty
    87	    # at this point in lifespan (in-process dict, fresh per worker)
    88	    # so any subdir under ``.agent_workspaces/`` is by definition
    89	    # orphan; the helper ``git worktree remove --force`` + ``shutil.
    90	    # rmtree`` fallback per orphan, emits ``workspace.orphan_cleanup``
    91	    # SSE + audit row per design §7 row 4. Best-effort — boot must
    92	    # not block on it (workspace recovery is observability, not a
    93	    # gate).
    94	    try:
    95	        from backend.workspace import cleanup_orphan_worktrees
    96	        orphans = await cleanup_orphan_worktrees()
    97	        if orphans:
    98	            log.warning(
    99	                "Startup cleanup: removed %d orphan worktree(s) "
   100	                "(paths=%s)",
   101	                len(orphans),
   102	                ", ".join(o["path"] for o in orphans),
   103	            )
   104	    except Exception as exc:
   105	        log.warning("Startup cleanup: orphan worktree scan failed: %s", exc)
   106	
   107	
   108	@asynccontextmanager
   109	async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
   110	    # Phase 52: configure structlog JSON output if requested
   111	    from backend import structlog_setup as _sl
   112	    _sl.configure()
   113	    import logging
   114	    _log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   115	    # OP-1455: bump the asyncio default executor to 32 worker threads.
   116	    # CPython's default is ``min(32, cpu+4)`` which is often 4-8 on
   117	    # production hosts. The /api/v1/project-state aggregator launches
   118	    # 3 axes concurrently, each wrapping blocking SQL/Cognee work in
   119	    # ``asyncio.to_thread``. When the slow structural axis (Cognee, ~5 s
   120	    # when KG is degraded) holds a thread for its full duration, the
   121	    # causal axis ``to_thread`` queues behind it and inherits the wait —
   122	    # OP-214 measured at 6.7 s in HTTP context vs 214 ms direct, because
   123	    # ``asyncio.wait_for`` can't cancel an already-running ``to_thread``
   124	    # future and has to drain the slow axis before re-raising
   125	    # TimeoutError. A 32-worker pool absorbs the three-axes × concurrent-
   126	    # request fan-out without contention.
   127	    import asyncio as _asyncio_init
   128	    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor as _TPE
   129	    try:
   130	        _asyncio_init.get_running_loop().set_default_executor(
   131	            _TPE(max_workers=32, thread_name_prefix="omnisight-to-thread")
   132	        )
   133	    except Exception as exc:
   134	        _log.warning("[startup] set_default_executor failed: %s", exc)
   135	    # L1-03: sanity-check critical env/config BEFORE opening the DB.
   136	    # Catches deploy-day typos (wrong bearer, missing provider key)
   137	    # at boot instead of the first 401 / first silent provider fail.
   138	    # Strict mode (refuse to boot on hard errors) auto-enables when
   139	    # debug=False; dev stays lenient.
   140	    from backend.config import validate_startup_config, ConfigValidationError
   141	    try:
   142	        validate_startup_config()
   143	    except ConfigValidationError as exc:
   144	        _log.error("config validation failed: %s", exc)
   145	        raise
   146	    try:
   147	        from backend.db import _resolve_pg_dsn as _r_pg
   148	        _pg_dsn = _r_pg()
   149	        if _pg_dsn:
   150	            from backend.alembic_startup_hook import (
   151	                AlembicBackwardDrift,
   152	                AlembicLockTimeout,
   153	                AlembicManifestError,
   154	                maybe_run_startup_upgrade,
   155	            )
   156	            try:
   157	                _db_head = maybe_run_startup_upgrade(db_url=_pg_dsn)
   158	                _log.info("alembic startup hook aligned DB head: %s", _db_head)
   159	            except AlembicBackwardDrift as exc:
   160	                _log.critical(
   161	                    json.dumps(exc.to_log_payload(), sort_keys=True)
   162	                )
   163	                sys.exit(78)
   164	            except AlembicLockTimeout as exc:
   165	                _log.critical(
   166	                    json.dumps(
   167	                        {
   168	                            "event": "alembic_lock_timeout",
   169	                            "remediation": (
   170	                                "wait for the active migration worker to "
   171	                                "finish or inspect stuck alembic lock holder"
   172	                            ),
   173	                            "error_msg": str(exc),
   174	                        },
   175	                        sort_keys=True,
   176	                    )
   177	                )
   178	                sys.exit(78)
   179	            except AlembicManifestError as exc:
   180	                _log.critical(
   181	                    json.dumps(
   182	                        {
   183	                            "event": "alembic_manifest_error",
   184	                            "remediation": (
   185	                                "rebuild image with /app/MANIFEST.json from "
   186	                                "OP-1159"
   187	                            ),
   188	                            "error_msg": str(exc),
   189	                        },
   190	                        sort_keys=True,
   191	                    )
   192	                )
   193	                sys.exit(78)
   194	        await db.init()
   195	        # Phase-3 Step C.2 (2026-04-21): the PG compat wrapper that
   196	        # used to coexist with the pool has been retired. On the PG
   197	        # path ``db.init()`` above is a no-op; the pool is the sole
   198	        # entry point. Gated on ``_resolve_pg_dsn()`` so SQLite dev /
   199	        # test flows (no DSN set) skip pool init and fall back to
   200	        # ``db._db`` for the handful of callers that still reach
   201	        # for aiosqlite directly.
   202	        from backend import db_pool as _db_pool
   203	        if _pg_dsn:
   204	            await _db_pool.init_pool(_pg_dsn)
   205	            _log.info("db_pool: initialised against PG DSN")
   206	        else:
   207	            _log.info(
   208	                "db_pool: skipped — no Postgres DSN in "
   209	                "OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL/DATABASE_URL (SQLite dev mode)"
   210	            )
   211	        await _startup_cleanup(_log)
   212	        # SP-3.1 (2026-04-20): agents.seed_defaults_if_empty was ported
   213	        # to require a pool-backed asyncpg connection. In SQLite dev mode
   214	        # the pool is absent, so we skip seeding with a clear warning —
   215	        # the in-memory _agents dict starts empty; operator creates
   216	        # agents via the UI instead. Epic 7 removes this branch when
   217	        # the compat wrapper is deleted.
   218	        if _pg_dsn:
   219	            async with _db_pool.get_pool().acquire() as _seed_conn:
   220	                await agents.seed_defaults_if_empty(_seed_conn)
     1	"""Phase 52 — Observability endpoints.
     2	
     3	GET  /metrics    Prometheus exposition format
     4	GET  /healthz    Deep health check (DB ping + version + watchdog age)
     5	
     6	Both endpoints are intentionally outside the authenticated surface so
     7	Prometheus / load-balancer health probes don't need credentials.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import asyncio
    13	import logging
    14	import time
    15	from typing import Any
    16	
    17	from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, HTTPException

    25	_HEALTHZ_TIMEOUT_S = 1.0

    29	# M7 audit (2026-04-19): optional token gate on /metrics. Today Next.js
    30	# rewrites only proxy `/api/v1/*`, so /metrics is unreachable from the
    31	# public internet — but (a) that's a rewrite-rule accident of silence,
    32	# not a security property, and (b) enabling the `observability`
    33	# compose profile (Prometheus) would make /metrics reachable from the
    34	# Prometheus container — fine, but ALSO by anything else that lands on
    35	# the same compose network, including future sidecars.
    36	#
    37	# With OMNISIGHT_METRICS_TOKEN set, /metrics requires either a
    38	# `?token=<value>` query arg or an `Authorization: Bearer <value>`
    39	# header, matched in constant time. Unset → endpoint stays open (the
    40	# in-cluster-only default stays backwards-compatible for operators
    41	# who haven't noticed this knob).
    42	def _check_metrics_token(request: Request) -> None:
    43	    from backend.config import settings as _settings
    44	    import secrets as _sec
    45	    expected = getattr(_settings, "metrics_token", "") or ""
    46	    if not expected:
    47	        return  # unset → open, matches pre-M7 behaviour
    48	    got = request.query_params.get("token") or ""
    49	    if not got:
    50	        auth = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
    51	        if auth.lower().startswith("bearer "):
    52	            got = auth[7:].strip()
    53	    if not _sec.compare_digest(got, expected):
    54	        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="metrics token required")
    55	
    56	
    57	@router.get("/metrics")
    58	async def get_metrics(request: Request) -> Response:
    59	    _check_metrics_token(request)
    60	    body, ctype = _metrics.render_exposition()
    61	    return Response(content=body, media_type=ctype)
    62	
    63	
    64	async def _probe_db() -> dict:
    65	    """Try a 1-statement query against the active DB."""
    66	    started = time.perf_counter()
    67	    try:
    68	        from backend.db_pool import get_pool
    69	        async with get_pool().acquire() as conn:
    70	            result = await conn.fetchval("SELECT 1")
    71	        ok = result == 1
    72	    except Exception as exc:
    73	        return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": int((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000),
    74	                "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc!s}"[:200]}
    75	    return {"ok": ok, "latency_ms": int((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)}
    76	
    77	
    78	def _watchdog_age_s() -> float | None:
    79	    """Best-effort age (seconds) since the watchdog last did a pass.
    80	    Returns None if the watchdog module hasn't started yet."""
    81	    try:
    82	        from backend.routers import invoke as _inv
    83	        last = getattr(_inv, "_watchdog_last_tick", None)
    84	        if not last:
    85	            return None
    86	        return time.time() - float(last)
    87	    except Exception as exc:
    88	        # Fix-B B5: not fatal, but shouldn't be completely silent.
    89	        logger.debug("_watchdog_age_s lookup failed: %s", exc)
    90	        return None
    91	
    92	
    93	@router.get("/healthz")
    94	async def healthz() -> JSONResponse:
    95	    """Deep health check.
    96	
    97	    Returns:
    98	      ok           overall — every component must report ok
    99	      version      backend semver
   100	      uptime_s     seconds since process start
   101	      auth_mode    open|session|strict
   102	      profile      currently active decision profile
   103	      db           {ok, latency_ms, error?}
   104	      watchdog     {age_s | null}
   105	      sse          {subscribers, dropped}
   106	    """
   107	    db_probe_task = asyncio.create_task(_probe_db())
   108	    try:
   109	        db_probe = await asyncio.wait_for(db_probe_task, timeout=_HEALTHZ_TIMEOUT_S)
   110	    except asyncio.TimeoutError:

    71	# CI-only to production. Earlier revisions kept these out of the
    72	# runtime image to save ~11 MB on wheels the backend-tests /
    73	# openapi-contract / renovate-config jobs didn't need; the cost of
    74	# that optimisation was that G4 [x] never meant "production can
    75	# actually talk to Postgres". ``asyncpg`` is the runtime driver
    76	# used by backend.db_connection::_PostgresAsyncConnection.
    77	# ``psycopg2-binary`` is the sync driver Alembic needs during
    78	# ``alembic upgrade head`` (SQLAlchemy sync engine required, asyncpg
    79	# not supported by Alembic's migration runner). Both stay optional
    80	# from a CODE perspective — backend.db_connection lazy-imports them
    81	# only when the target URL is a ``postgresql+*`` scheme; the plain
    82	# SQLite path keeps working on deployments that don't set

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# scripts/deployment-audit.sh — [OP-976] AUDIT-23
     3	#
     4	# Re-runnable "shipped vs deployed" verification harness. For each row in an
     5	# expected-live manifest it applies the AUDIT-23 §4 verification methodology
     6	# and prints a green/red table; exits non-zero if any row marked `expected=yes`
     7	# is red. Designed to be the standing regression guard against the
     8	# "shipped-but-not-deployed" anti-pattern (see
     9	# docs/audit/2026-05-12-shipped-not-deployed-sprint-dEF.md §6.3).
    10	#
    11	# Run it ON THE HOST that owns the artefacts (the prod host for the release
    12	# units; the sora-bridge host for the bridge units). Re-run quarterly minimum,
    13	# or wrap it in deploy/systemd/deployment-audit.{service,timer} for a daily run.
    14	#
    15	# Usage:
    16	#   scripts/deployment-audit.sh                 # built-in expected-live list
    17	#   scripts/deployment-audit.sh MANIFEST.tsv    # host-specific manifest file
    18	#   DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_USER_SYSTEMD=0 scripts/deployment-audit.sh   # use system bus
    19	#   DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_JSONL_LOG=/path/audit.jsonl scripts/deployment-audit.sh
    20	#
    21	# Manifest format — tab-separated, `#` comments and blank lines ignored:
    22	#   <kind>  <name>  <expected>  <ticket>  [note]
    23	#
    24	#   kind ∈ {
    25	#     systemd-unit     name = unit (e.g. auto-promote-main.service)
    26	#     systemd-timer    name = timer (e.g. release-milestone-checker.timer); also
    27	#                      checks the bound service's last Result + list-timers LAST
    28	#     container        name = docker name substring, or  substr@http://host:port/healthz
    29	#     env-var          name = VAR@unit.service   or   VAR@pgrep-pattern
    30	#     alembic-head     name = expected-revision   or   "auto" (compare vs `alembic heads`)
    31	#   }
    32	#   expected ∈ { yes, gated, n-a }
    33	#     yes   → a red row makes this script exit 1
    34	#     gated → peer-gated by design (e.g. staging-gate timers); red is reported, not fatal
    35	#     n-a   → informational only
    36	#
    37	# Exit: 0 = every `expected=yes` row green · 1 = ≥1 red · 2 = bad usage / no host.
    38	
    39	set -uo pipefail
    40	
    41	REPO="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
    42	USE_USER_BUS="${DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_USER_SYSTEMD:-1}"
    43	SYSTEMCTL=(systemctl)
    44	[ "$USE_USER_BUS" = "1" ] && SYSTEMCTL=(systemctl --user)
    45	
    46	err()  { echo "❌ deployment-audit: $*" >&2; exit 2; }
    47	have() { command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
    48	
    49	looks_prod_value() {
    50	  local val="$1"
    51	  [ -n "$val" ] && ! echo "$val" | grep -qiE 'sqlite|localhost|127\.0\.0\.1|placeholder|changeme'
    52	}
    53	
    54	# ── results accumulator ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    55	FAIL=0          # number of red rows with expected=yes
    56	ROWS=()         # "status|kind|name|expected|ticket|detail"
    57	record() {      # record <STATUS> <kind> <name> <expected> <ticket> <detail>
    58	  local status="$1" kind="$2" name="$3" exp="$4" ticket="$5" detail="$6"
    59	  ROWS+=("${status}|${kind}|${name}|${exp}|${ticket}|${detail}")
    60	  if [ "$status" = "RED" ] && [ "$exp" = "yes" ]; then FAIL=$((FAIL + 1)); fi
    61	}
    62	
    63	# ── prerequisite: linger (all --user units die at logout without it) ──────────
    64	check_linger() {
    65	  [ "$USE_USER_BUS" = "1" ] || return 0
    66	  have loginctl || { record "WARN" "linger" "$USER" "n-a" "OP-976" "loginctl absent — cannot verify"; return 0; }
    67	  local v; v="$(loginctl show-user "$USER" -p Linger --value 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"
    68	  if [ "$v" = "yes" ]; then
    69	    record "OK" "linger" "$USER" "yes" "OP-976" "Linger=yes"
    70	  else
    71	    record "RED" "linger" "$USER" "yes" "OP-976" "Linger=$v — every --user timer stops at logout; run: loginctl enable-linger $USER"
    72	  fi
    73	}
    74	
    75	# ── per-kind checks ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    76	check_systemd_unit() {  # name expected ticket
    77	  local name="$1" exp="$2" ticket="$3"
    78	  have systemctl || { record "WARN" "systemd-unit" "$name" "$exp" "$ticket" "systemctl absent"; return; }
    79	  local active enabled load
    80	  load="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$name" -p LoadState --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
    81	  if [ "$load" = "not-found" ]; then
    82	    record "RED" "systemd-unit" "$name" "$exp" "$ticket" "unit not installed (no $name in the user/systemd manager) — copy from deploy/systemd/ + daemon-reload + enable --now"
    83	    return
    84	  fi
    85	  active="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" is-active   "$name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    86	  enabled="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" is-enabled "$name" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    87	  if [ "$active" = "active" ] && [ "$enabled" = "enabled" ]; then
    88	    record "OK" "systemd-unit" "$name" "$exp" "$ticket" "active+enabled"
    89	  else
    90	    record "RED" "systemd-unit" "$name" "$exp" "$ticket" "is-active=$active is-enabled=$enabled"
    91	  fi
    92	}
    93	
    94	check_systemd_timer() {  # name expected ticket
    95	  local timer="$1" exp="$2" ticket="$3"
    96	  have systemctl || { record "WARN" "systemd-timer" "$timer" "$exp" "$ticket" "systemctl absent"; return; }
    97	  local enabled svc last result load
    98	  load="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$timer" -p LoadState --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
    99	  if [ "$load" = "not-found" ]; then
   100	    record "RED" "systemd-timer" "$timer" "$exp" "$ticket" "timer not installed — copy deploy/systemd/${timer%.timer}.{service,timer} to ~/.config/systemd/user/ + daemon-reload + enable --now"
   101	    return
   102	  fi
   103	  enabled="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" is-enabled "$timer" 2>/dev/null || true)"
   104	  svc="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$timer" -p Unit --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
   105	  [ -n "$svc" ] || svc="${timer%.timer}.service"
   106	  last="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" list-timers --all 2>/dev/null | awk -v t="$timer" '$0 ~ t {print "LAST="$5" "$6}' | head -1)"
   107	  result="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$svc" -p Result --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
   108	  if [ "$enabled" = "enabled" ] && { [ "$result" = "success" ] || [ -z "$result" ]; }; then
   109	    record "OK" "systemd-timer" "$timer" "$exp" "$ticket" "enabled; ${svc} Result=${result:-n/a}; ${last:-no-list-timers-row}"
   110	  else
   111	    record "RED" "systemd-timer" "$timer" "$exp" "$ticket" "is-enabled=$enabled; ${svc} Result=${result:-n/a}; ${last:-no-list-timers-row}"
   112	  fi
   113	}
   114	
   115	check_container() {  # name(substr[@probeurl]) expected ticket
   116	  local spec="$1" exp="$2" ticket="$3"
   117	  local substr="${spec%@*}" probe=""
   118	  [ "$spec" != "$substr" ] && probe="${spec#*@}"
   119	  have docker || { record "WARN" "container" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "docker absent"; return; }
   120	  local line; line="$(docker ps --format '{{.Names}}\t{{.Status}}' 2>/dev/null | grep -i -- "$substr" | head -1)"
   121	  if [ -z "$line" ]; then
   122	    record "RED" "container" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "no running container matching '$substr' (never \`docker compose up\`?)"
   123	    return
   124	  fi
   125	  local status="${line#*$'\t'}"
   126	  if [ -n "$probe" ] && have curl; then
   127	    if curl -fsS -m 5 "$probe" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   128	      record "OK" "container" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$line; probe $probe → 2xx"
   129	    else
   130	      record "RED" "container" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$line; probe $probe FAILED"
   131	    fi
   132	  elif echo "$status" | grep -qi 'unhealthy'; then
   133	    record "RED" "container" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$line (unhealthy)"
   134	  else
   135	    record "OK" "container" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$line"
   136	  fi
   137	}
   138	
   139	check_env_var() {  # name(VAR@unit-or-pgrep) expected ticket
   140	  local spec="$1" exp="$2" ticket="$3"
   141	  local var="${spec%@*}" src="${spec#*@}"
   142	  [ "$spec" != "$var" ] || { record "WARN" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "spec must be VAR@unit-or-pgrep"; return; }
   143	  local pid=""
   144	  if [[ "$src" == *.service ]] && have systemctl; then
   145	    local env_line env_files file_path file_val
   146	    env_line="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$src" -p Environment --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
   147	    file_val="$(printf '%s\n' "$env_line" | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -E "^${var}=" | head -1 | cut -d= -f2- || true)"
   148	    if [ -n "$file_val" ]; then
   149	      if looks_prod_value "$file_val"; then
   150	        record "OK" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var configured in systemd Environment"
   151	      else
   152	        record "RED" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var configured in systemd Environment but looks like a dev/default value"
   153	      fi
   154	      return
   155	    fi
   156	    env_files="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$src" -p EnvironmentFiles --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
   157	    while IFS= read -r file_line; do
   158	      file_path="${file_line%% *}"
   159	      [ -r "$file_path" ] || continue
   160	      file_val="$(grep -E "^${var}=" "$file_path" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d= -f2- || true)"
   161	      [ -n "$file_val" ] || continue
   162	      if looks_prod_value "$file_val"; then
   163	        record "OK" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var configured via ${file_path}"
   164	      else
   165	        record "RED" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var configured via ${file_path} but looks like a dev/default value"
   166	      fi
   167	      return
   168	    done <<<"$env_files"
   169	    pid="$("${SYSTEMCTL[@]}" show "$src" -p MainPID --value 2>/dev/null || true)"
   170	    [ "$pid" = "0" ] && pid=""
   171	  fi
   172	  if [ -z "$pid" ] && have pgrep; then pid="$(pgrep -f -- "$src" 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)"; fi
   173	  if [ -z "$pid" ]; then
   174	    record "RED" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "no live PID for '$src' — cannot inspect /proc/<pid>/environ"
   175	    return
   176	  fi
   177	  if [ ! -r "/proc/$pid/environ" ]; then
   178	    record "WARN" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "PID $pid found but /proc/$pid/environ not readable (run as the owning user / root)"
   179	    return
   180	  fi
   181	  local val; val="$(tr '\0' '\n' < "/proc/$pid/environ" 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^${var}=" | head -1 | cut -d= -f2-)"
   182	  if [ -z "$val" ]; then
   183	    record "RED" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var not set in PID $pid env (silent fallback / default in effect)"
   184	  elif ! looks_prod_value "$val"; then
   185	    record "RED" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var=$val (PID $pid) — looks like a dev/default value, not the prod target"
   186	  else
   187	    record "OK" "env-var" "$spec" "$exp" "$ticket" "$var set in PID $pid (=${val%%:*}...)"
   188	  fi
   189	}
   190	
   191	check_alembic_head() {  # name(expected-rev|"auto") expected ticket
   192	  local want="$1" exp="$2" ticket="$3"
   193	  local alembic_cmd="alembic"
   194	  [ -x "$REPO/backend/.venv/bin/alembic" ] && alembic_cmd="$REPO/backend/.venv/bin/alembic"
   195	  [ "$alembic_cmd" = "alembic" ] && [ -x "$HOME/.local/bin/alembic" ] && alembic_cmd="$HOME/.local/bin/alembic"
   196	  have "$alembic_cmd" || { record "WARN" "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "alembic absent — run from the backend venv on prod"; return; }
   197	  [ -r "$REPO/backend/alembic.ini" ] || { record "RED" "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "backend/alembic.ini missing"; return; }
   198	  # Read PROD's live PG head first (the audit shell has no prod DSN, so a bare
   199	  # `alembic current` reads the stale local sqlite and falsely reports drift).
   200	  # Fall back to local alembic only if the prod PG container is unreachable.
   201	  local pg_ctr="${OMNISIGHT_PROD_PG_CONTAINER:-omnisight-pg-primary}"
   202	  local cur; cur="$(docker exec "$pg_ctr" psql -U "${OMNISIGHT_PROD_PG_USER:-omnisight}" -d "${OMNISIGHT_PROD_PG_DB:-omnisight}" -tA -c 'SELECT version_num FROM alembic_version' 2>/dev/null | grep -oE '^[0-9a-f]{4,}' | head -1 || true)"
   203	  [ -n "$cur" ] || cur="$( (cd "$REPO/backend" && "$alembic_cmd" current 2>/dev/null) | grep -oE '^[0-9a-f]{4,}' | head -1 || true)"
   204	  [ -n "$cur" ] || { record "RED" "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "prod PG ($pg_ctr) unreachable AND local alembic empty"; return; }
   205	  if [ "$want" = "auto" ]; then
   206	    local head; head="$( (cd "$REPO/backend" && "$alembic_cmd" heads 2>/dev/null) | grep -oE '^[0-9a-f]{4,}' | head -1 || true)"
   207	    if [ "$cur" = "$head" ]; then record "OK"  "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "current=$cur == repo head"
   208	    else                          record "RED" "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "current=$cur != repo head=$head — un-applied migration; run \`alembic upgrade head\` on prod"; fi
   209	  else
   210	    if [ "$cur" = "$want" ]; then record "OK"  "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "current=$cur"
   211	    else                          record "RED" "alembic-head" "$want" "$exp" "$ticket" "current=$cur != expected=$want"; fi
   212	  fi
   213	}
   214	
   215	append_jsonl() {
   216	  local result="$1" ok="$2" red="$3" warn="$4" log="${DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_JSONL_LOG:-}"
   217	  [ -n "$log" ] || return 0
   218	  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$log")" || return 0
   219	  {
   220	    printf '%s|%s|%s|%s|%s\n' "$result" "$ok" "$red" "$warn" "$FAIL"
   250	verify_args=()
   251	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = true ]; then
   252	    verify_args+=("--allowlist-only")
   253	fi
   254	
   255	if [ -n "$DIGEST" ]; then
   256	    # Digest deploy: the image is already built and cosign-verified at
   257	    # this digest (RT-06), so there is no git ref to fetch or check out.
   258	    # Force --skip-build — the image carries the code + Alembic
   259	    # migrations (the alembic step below runs inside that image, not the
   260	    # working tree). Compose pull-by-digest via OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST is
   261	    # wired in RT-07b; here we record the digest as the deploy identity.
   262	    scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh --kind digest --ref "$DIGEST" "${verify_args[@]}"
   263	    SKIP_BUILD=true
   264	    _upsert_env "OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST" "$DIGEST"
   265	    log "Digest deploy: $DIGEST (skip-build；不做 git checkout)"
   266	else
   267	    GERRIT_SOURCE="$(_detect_gerrit_source)"
   268	    echo "Git source: $GERRIT_SOURCE"
   269	    _run_cmd git fetch "$GERRIT_SOURCE" --tags
   270	    scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh --kind tag --ref "$TAG" "${verify_args[@]}"
   271	    _run_cmd git checkout "$TAG"
   272	    log "Code 更新完成：$(git log --oneline -1)"
   273	fi
   274	
   275	# OP-772: expose current/previous image tags to the persistent SLO monitor
   276	# before any replica is restarted. The monitor uses the previous tag as
   277	# its rollback target if three consecutive 30 s SLO windows breach.
   278	CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG="${OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG:-${TAG:-$DIGEST}}"
   279	PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG="$(grep -E '^OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG=' .env 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d= -f2- || true)"
   280	if [ -n "${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG:-}" ] && [ "$PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG" != "$CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG" ]; then
   281	    _upsert_env "OMNISIGHT_PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG" "$PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG"
   282	fi
   283	_upsert_env "OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG" "$CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG"
   284	log "SLO monitor image tags: current=$CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG previous=${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG:-unknown}"
   285	
   286	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = false ]; then
   287	    if systemctl --user list-unit-files omnisight-slo-monitor.service >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   288	        systemctl --user restart omnisight-slo-monitor.service || \
   289	            warn "omnisight-slo-monitor.service restart failed before deploy — check systemd user logs"
   290	    else
   291	        warn "omnisight-slo-monitor.service not installed — install deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service for OP-772"
   292	    fi
   293	else
   294	    echo "  [dry-run] systemctl --user restart omnisight-slo-monitor.service"
   295	fi

   299	# data integrity if a migration blows up or a code change panics on
   300	# existing rows. The `scripts/backup_prod_db.sh` helper takes a WAL-

   308	    else

   280	# ── main ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   281	main() {
   282	  local manifest_src
   283	  if [ $# -ge 1 ]; then
   284	    [ -r "$1" ] || err "manifest '$1' not readable"
   285	    manifest_src="$(cat "$1")"
   286	  else
   287	    manifest_src="$(builtin_manifest)"
   288	  fi
   289	
   290	  echo "deployment-audit (OP-976 / AUDIT-23) — host=$(hostname) user=$USER bus=$([ "$USE_USER_BUS" = 1 ] && echo --user || echo system) date=$(date -u +%FT%TZ)"
   291	  echo
   292	
   293	  check_linger
   294	
   295	  while IFS= read -r raw; do
   296	    raw="${raw%%$'\r'}"
   297	    case "$raw" in ''|\#*) continue;; esac
   298	    # split on runs of whitespace into up to 5 fields (note may contain spaces)
   299	    read -r kind name expected ticket note <<<"$raw"
   300	    [ -n "${kind:-}" ] && [ -n "${name:-}" ] || { echo "  (skipping malformed manifest line: $raw)" >&2; continue; }
   301	    expected="${expected:-yes}"; ticket="${ticket:--}"
   302	    case "$kind" in
   303	      systemd-unit)  check_systemd_unit  "$name" "$expected" "$ticket" ;;
   304	      systemd-timer) check_systemd_timer "$name" "$expected" "$ticket" ;;
   305	      container)     check_container     "$name" "$expected" "$ticket" ;;
   306	      env-var)       check_env_var       "$name" "$expected" "$ticket" ;;
   307	      alembic-head)  check_alembic_head  "$name" "$expected" "$ticket" ;;
   308	      *)             record "WARN" "$kind" "$name" "$expected" "$ticket" "unknown kind" ;;
   309	    esac
   310	  done <<<"$manifest_src"
   311	
   312	  # ── report ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   313	  echo
   314	  printf '%-5s  %-14s  %-58s  %-8s  %-9s  %s\n' STATUS KIND NAME EXPECTED TICKET DETAIL
   315	  printf '%-5s  %-14s  %-58s  %-8s  %-9s  %s\n' '-----' '--------------' '----------------------------------------------------------' '--------' '---------' '------'
   316	  local r status kind name exp ticket detail mark
   317	  for r in "${ROWS[@]}"; do
   318	    IFS='|' read -r status kind name exp ticket detail <<<"$r"
   319	    case "$status" in
   320	      OK)   mark='✓ OK  ' ;;
   321	      RED)  mark='✗ RED ' ;;
   322	      WARN) mark='? WARN' ;;
   323	      *)    mark="$status" ;;
   324	    esac
   325	    printf '%-5s  %-14s  %-58s  %-8s  %-9s  %s\n' "$mark" "$kind" "$name" "$exp" "$ticket" "$detail"
   326	  done
   327	  echo
   328	  local ok red warn
   329	  ok=$(  printf '%s\n' "${ROWS[@]}" | grep -c '^OK|'   || true)
   330	  red=$( printf '%s\n' "${ROWS[@]}" | grep -c '^RED|'  || true)
   331	  warn=$(printf '%s\n' "${ROWS[@]}" | grep -c '^WARN|' || true)
   332	  echo "summary: ${ok} green · ${red} red · ${warn} warn · ${FAIL} red-with-expected=yes (fatal)"
   333	  if [ "$FAIL" -gt 0 ]; then
   334	    append_jsonl "FAIL" "$ok" "$red" "$warn"
   335	    echo "RESULT: FAIL — $FAIL expected-live artefact(s) not deployed. See DETAIL column; remediation in docs/audit/2026-05-12-shipped-not-deployed-sprint-dEF.md §5."
   336	    exit 1
   337	  fi
   338	  append_jsonl "PASS" "$ok" "$red" "$warn"
   339	  echo "RESULT: PASS — all expected-live artefacts confirmed (gated/warn rows are informational)."
   340	  exit 0

    32	## 🔴🟡 Gaps / drift (what needs fixing)
    33	
    34	**G1 (BLOCKER) — NOT DEPLOYED.** `omnisight-slo-monitor.service` = `enabled=not-found` /
    35	`inactive` on the host; `omnisight-canary*.service` likewise. NOT in
    36	`scripts/deployment-audit.sh` manifest → the gap is invisible to the daily audit. No
    37	live auto-rollback exists today.
    38	
    39	**G2 (BLOCKER) — METRIC CONTRACT MISMATCH → silent always-healthy.** Both monitors query
    40	`http_requests_total{route=...}` + `http_request_duration_seconds_bucket` (slo_monitor.py
    41	`_fetch_route`). But the app exports **domain** metrics only (`decision_total`,
    42	`pipeline_step_seconds`, `provider_*`, … in `backend/metrics.py`, served at
    43	`/api/v1/metrics`) — there is **no instrumentator and no `http_requests_total`
    44	definition anywhere** (only the 2 monitors + staging_validation *query* it). So every
    45	PromQL returns empty → `total_value <= 0` → `_fetch_route` returns
    46	`RouteWindow(error_rate=0, success_rate=1.0)` = **healthy** → no breach ever → **no
    47	rollback ever.** Even deployed, it is a dead safety net (worse: false confidence).
    48	
    49	**G3 (SHOULD-FIX) — scrape-target drift.** `configs/prometheus.yml` scrapes
    50	`targets: ["backend:8000"]`, but prod runs `backend-a` + `backend-b` (no `backend`
    51	service). Even after G2, the scrape may hit nothing.
    52	
    53	**G4 (SHOULD-FIX) — DUPLICATE implementations; the unit runs the weaker one.** The
    54	systemd unit runs OP-772 `backend/slo_monitor.py` (compose full-rollback only; **no
    55	cooldown, no measurement-plane-dark guard**). The richer OP-883
    56	`backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py` (cooldown + **refuse-rollback-when-blind** +
    57	canary/full decision) is NOT wired to the unit. Critically, OP-883's
    58	refuse-when-blind guard is exactly what makes G2 safe-ish (a blind monitor that
    59	refuses to act beats one that reads-healthy). Decide the canonical impl + wire it.
    60	
    61	**G5 (SHOULD-FIX) — rollback-target coupling to deploy-prod.sh.** `previous_tag` =
    62	`OMNISIGHT_PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG`, written ONLY by `deploy-prod.sh` (:281). The release-train
    63	handoff step 7 says "deploy prod by the existing prod SOP" — if prod is deployed via the
    64	manual SOP / release-train promote rather than `deploy-prod.sh`, no previous_tag is
    65	written → rollback has no target → no-op. Verify the prod deploy path.
    66	
    67	**G6 (NICE) — canary branch is dead weight.** OP-883's canary-vs-full decision keys on
    68	`canary_state.json` + the D9/D10 canary orchestrator, but progressive prod canary
    69	(OP-931/932/933) was HOLD'd/Archived → canary_state.json never exists → always full
    70	rollback. The canary code path is unreachable in the current single-env topology.
    71	
    72	**G7 (NICE) — SLO threshold spec inconsistency.** OP-772 (error<0.5% / success>99.5% /
    73	p95<500ms) vs OP-883 (error<1% / p95<500ms). `config/slos.yaml` is the source of truth —
    74	reconcile + confirm which thresholds are live.
    75	
    76	## Recommended fix scope (ordered; activation BLOCKED until G2 fixed)
    77	1. **G2 (must, first):** make the HTTP-metric contract real — add an ASGI Prometheus
    78	   instrumentator exporting `http_requests_total{route,status}` +
    79	   `http_request_duration_seconds_bucket{route,le}` at `/api/v1/metrics`, OR rewrite the
    80	   monitor's PromQL to existing domain metrics. (Instrumentator is the right fix — these
    81	   are standard RED metrics the SLO spec assumes.)
    82	2. **G4:** pick the OP-883 orchestrator monitor as canonical (it has the

    86	3. **G3:** fix the scrape target to backend-a + backend-b (or a shared alias).

    91	6. G6/G7: drop/guard the dead canary branch; reconcile slos.yaml thresholds.

   230	def load_thresholds(path: Path | str = DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH) -> SloThresholds:
   231	    """Parse ``config/slo_thresholds.yaml`` into :class:`SloThresholds`.
   232	
   233	    Missing keys fall back to the dataclass defaults so a partial YAML
   234	    keeps booting; an unparseable file raises so we don't silently run
   235	    on stale numbers.
   236	    """

   238	    defaults = SloThresholds()
   239	    return SloThresholds(
   240	        error_rate_max=float(raw.get("error_rate_max", defaults.error_rate_max)),
   241	        p95_latency_ms_max=float(

   252	        ),

   254	            raw.get(
   255	                "graphiti_query_p95_ms_max",
   256	                defaults.graphiti_query_p95_ms_max,
   257	            )

   430	
   431	    def maybe_rollback(self, reason: str) -> RollbackOutcome:
   432	        """Operator-driven entrypoint (called from the runbook script).
   433	
   434	        Respects the cooldown gate -- raises :class:`CooldownInEffect`
   435	        when invoked inside the AC #4 window so two operators can't
   436	        race a manual rollback against the auto path.
   437	        """
   438	        now = self.clock()
   439	        if now < self._cooldown_until:
   440	            remaining = self._cooldown_until - now
   441	            raise CooldownInEffect(
   442	                f"cooldown active for {remaining:.0f}s more"
   443	            )
   444	        outcome = self.rollback.trigger(reason=reason)
   445	        self._cooldown_until = now + self.thresholds.cooldown_seconds
   446	        return outcome
   447	
   448	    def run_forever(self, *, max_iterations: int | None = None) -> None:
   449	        """Daemon loop. ``max_iterations`` is for the daemonised tests
   450	        that don't want a true infinite loop."""
   451	        iterations = 0
   452	        while True:
   453	            try:
   454	                self.tick()
   455	            except Exception:  # pragma: no cover -- defensive
   456	                logger.exception("SLO monitor tick raised")
   457	            iterations += 1
   458	            if max_iterations is not None and iterations >= max_iterations:
   459	                return
   460	            self.sleeper(self.thresholds.sample_interval_seconds)
   461	
   462	    # ── internal ──
   463

   466	        for name, sample_attr, threshold_attr in CROSS_TASK_SLOS:
   467	            value = float(getattr(sample, sample_attr))
   468	            threshold = float(getattr(self.thresholds, threshold_attr))
   469	            status[name] = {
   470	                "value_ms": value,
   640	
   641	
   642	def build_default_rollback() -> RollbackTrigger:
   643	    """Wire D10 canary abort + D9 full prod rollback.
   644	
   645	    The canary side defers to :mod:`backend.canary_rollout` (its
   646	    ``manual_control(state, "abort")``); the full side defers to
   647	    :mod:`backend.production_release`. Both are kept inside the closure
   648	    so importing this module from tests does not eagerly load the
   649	    deploy plumbing.
   650	    """
   651	
   652	    def _canary_active() -> bool:
   653	        from backend import canary_rollout as cr
   654	        try:
   655	            state = cr.load_state()
   656	        except FileNotFoundError:
   657	            return False
   658	        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover -- corrupt state -> no canary
   659	            logger.exception("canary state load failed; assuming no canary")
   660	            return False
   661	        return state.status in {"running", "paused"}
   662	
   663	    def _canary_trigger(reason: str) -> RollbackOutcome:
   664	        from backend import canary_rollout as cr
   665	        state = cr.load_state()
   666	        controller = cr.CanaryController()
   667	        new_state = controller.manual_control(
   668	            state, "abort", reason=reason,
   669	        )
   670	        return RollbackOutcome(
   671	            mode="canary",
   672	            status="aborted",
   673	            detail=f"rollout_id={new_state.rollout_id} status={new_state.status}",
   674	        )
   675	
   676	    def _full_trigger(reason: str) -> RollbackOutcome:
   677	        from backend import production_release
   678	        tag = os.environ.get(
   679	            "OMNISIGHT_PROD_CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG", "current",
   680	        )
   681	        orch = production_release.ProductionDeployOrchestrator()
   682	        orch._rollback(tag)  # noqa: SLF001 -- intentional: D9 hook
   683	        return RollbackOutcome(
   684	            mode="full", status="rolled_back",
   685	            detail=f"tag={tag} reason={reason}",
   686	        )
   687	
   688	    return _CompositeRollbackTrigger(
   689	        canary_trigger=_canary_trigger,
   690	        full_trigger=_full_trigger,
   691	        canary_active=_canary_active,
   692	    )

   360	class SloMonitor:
   361	    def __init__(
   362	        self,
   363	        *,
   364	        config: SloConfig,
   365	        source: MetricWindowSource,
   366	        rollback: ComposeRollbackExecutor,
   367	        clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
   368	        sleeper: Callable[[float], Any] = time.sleep,
   369	    ) -> None:
   370	        self.config = config
   371	        self.source = source
   372	        self.rollback = rollback
   373	        self.detector = ConsecutiveBreachDetector(config)
   374	        self.clock = clock
   375	        self.sleeper = sleeper
   376	
   377	    def run_for(self, duration_seconds: int) -> RollbackResult | None:
   378	        deadline = self.clock() + duration_seconds
   379	        while self.clock() < deadline:
   380	            breaches = self.detector.observe(self.source.fetch(self.config.interval_seconds))
   381	            if breaches:
   382	                asyncio.run(notify_slo_breach(breaches[0]))
   383	                result = self.rollback.rollback(self.config.rollback_max_seconds)
   384	                asyncio.run(notify_rollback(result))
   385	                return result
   386	            self.sleeper(self.config.interval_seconds)
   387	        return None
   388

   402	    args = build_arg_parser().parse_args(argv)
   403	    config = load_slo_config(args.config)
   404	    duration = args.duration_seconds or config.post_deploy_monitor_seconds
   405	    source = PrometheusMetricSource(
   406	        args.prometheus_url,

   408	    )

   410	        previous_tag=args.previous_tag,

   412	    )

   414	    if result is None:
   415	        logger.info("SLO monitor finished without breach")
   416	        return 0
   417	    return 0 if result.status == "rolled_back" else 2

   500	                "axes": axis_status,
   501	            },

   504	
   505	    def _fire_rollback(
   506	        self, *, sample: SloSample, breached: tuple[str, ...],
   507	    ) -> RollbackOutcome:
   508	        reason = (
   509	            "SLO breach sustained >"
   510	            f"{self.thresholds.breach_sustain_seconds}s on "
   511	            + ",".join(breached)
   512	        )
   513	        mode = self.rollback.mode
   514	        # SSE first so the dashboard sees the breach even if the
   515	        # rollback hangs on a Docker/Caddy retry. Payload mirrors the
   516	        # OP-761 §AC field set so the React side can render without
   517	        # peeking at the rollback subevent.
   518	        events.bus.publish(
   519	            "slo.breach",
   520	            {
   521	                "error_rate": sample.error_rate,
   522	                "p95_latency_ms": sample.p95_latency_ms,
   523	                "error_rate_threshold": self.thresholds.error_rate_max,
   524	                "p95_latency_ms_threshold": self.thresholds.p95_latency_ms_max,
   525	                "axes": self._axis_status(sample),
   526	                "breached_metrics": list(breached),
   527	                "rollback_mode": mode,
   528	                "reason": reason,
   529	            },
   530	            broadcast_scope="global",
   531	        )
   532	        # Page operator (best-effort -- never block the rollback).
   533	        if self.notify is not None:
   534	            try:
   535	                self.notify(
   536	                    "SLO breach detected -- auto-rollback firing",
   537	                    reason,
   538	                )
   539	            except Exception:  # pragma: no cover -- defensive
   540	                logger.exception("SLO monitor notify hook raised")
   541	        try:
   542	            outcome = self.rollback.trigger(reason=reason)
   543	        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover -- defensive
   544	            logger.exception("SLO monitor rollback trigger failed")
   545	            return RollbackOutcome(
   546	                mode=mode, status="failed", detail=str(exc),
   547	            )
   548	        return outcome

   110	
   111	# Shared aggregate routers — exported for tests that pin the per-version
   112	# contract shape via _schema_for(...) without spinning up the full app.
   113	api_v1_router = APIRouter()
   114	api_v2_router = APIRouter()
   115	
   116	
   117	def api_relative_path(path: str, fallback_prefix: str) -> str:
   118	    """Return the route path after stripping any supported API version prefix.
   119	
   120	    Pre-OP-774 the middleware gates compared ``request.url.path`` against
   121	    ``settings.api_prefix`` only. With v1/v2 prefixes co-existing, that
   122	    compare would miss ``/api/v2/...`` entirely and the gates would
   123	    silently stop applying. This helper is the single source of truth
   124	    used by every gate.
   125	    """
   126	    for prefix in (API_V1_PREFIX, API_V2_PREFIX, fallback_prefix):
   127	        if path == prefix:
   128	            return "/"
   129	        if path.startswith(prefix + "/"):
   130	            return path.removeprefix(prefix)
   131	    return path
   132	
   133	
   134	def include_versioned_router(router: APIRouter) -> None:
   135	    """Add ``router`` to both v1 and v2 aggregate surfaces.
   136	
   137	    Used internally by :func:`register_versioned_api`; exposed for the
   138	    rare caller (tests) that wants to add a router without going through
   139	    the bulk-register path.
   140	    """
   141	    api_v1_router.include_router(router)
   142	    api_v2_router.include_router(router)
   143

**Verdict: needs-revision**

Prioritized fixes before filing:

- **MIS-SPEC: F1 instrumentator contract is too loose.** The monitors require `http_requests_total` and `http_request_duration_seconds_bucket` exactly. OP-772 also requires `route` and `status` labels for per-route/error queries: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/slo_monitor.py:129-136`. OP-883 requires same metric names and `status` for errors, but no route label: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:595-603`. A standard FastAPI instrumentator commonly emits `handler`, not `route`, and may group status codes. The ticket must say the implementation must either configure/adapt the instrumentator or custom-register metrics with the exact monitor labels. Do not imply a default instrumentator will match.

- **MIS-SPEC: F1’s 5xx AC should not hardcode `status="500"` unless the chosen exporter is configured that way.** OP-772/OP-883 query `status=~"5.."`, which matches `500`, not grouped `5xx`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/slo_monitor.py:132`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:597-598`. If using grouped status labels, monitor PromQL must change.

- **OK with important constraint: F1 must register into the existing custom registry.** `/api/v1/metrics` renders `backend.metrics.REGISTRY`, not the default global registry: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/routers/observability.py:57-61`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/metrics.py:46-48`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/metrics.py:962-966`. The draft correctly calls this out at draft lines 28-31.

- **MIS-SPEC: F2 line refs for OP-883 safety are wrong.** Cooldown is `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:347-355`, suppress is `:357-364`, MetricSourceUnavailable handling is `:372-381`, not `:419` as draft line 61-62 states. `:419` is cooldown arming after rollback.

- **OK: OP-883 is runnable as `python -m backend.orchestrator.slo_monitor`.** It has `main()` and `if __name__ == "__main__"`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:739-756`.

- **OK: current unit is stale and runs OP-772.** Current `WorkingDirectory` is `%h/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer`, env file is same tree, and `ExecStart` is `.../backend/.venv/bin/python -m backend.slo_monitor`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service:14-17`.

- **CONVENTION / SCOPE: retiring `backend/slo_monitor.py` is risky unless tests/docs are updated or shimmed.** Code imports are mostly tests, but docs and the unit reference it: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/tests/test_slo_monitor.py:10`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/operations/slo-monitor-rollback.md:10`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/operations/release-runbook.md:322`. A shim is safer than deletion.

- **OK: two threshold files exist and are read by different monitors.** OP-772 reads `config/slos.yaml`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/slo_monitor.py:30`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/config/slos.yaml:1-18`. OP-883 reads `config/slo_thresholds.yaml`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:82`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:230-284`, `/home/user/sora-bridge/config/slo_thresholds.yaml:1-31`.

- **OK: F3 target diagnosis is correct.** Prometheus currently scrapes only `backend:8000`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/configs/prometheus.yml:7-10`. `backend-a` has the legacy `backend` alias: `/home/user/sora-bridge/docker-compose.prod.yml:195-218`. `backend-b` listens on port `8001`, not `8000`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/docker-compose.prod.yml:269-285`. So F3’s AC should be `backend-a:8000` + `backend-b:8001`, not `backend-b:8000`.

- **OK: F4 manifest format and deploy-prod wiring.** Manifest rows are whitespace-separated `<kind> <name> <expected> <ticket> [note]`: `/home/user/sora-bridge/scripts/deployment-audit.sh:21-35`, parsed at `:295-308`. Built-in rows live at `:263-277`; add `systemd-unit omnisight-slo-monitor.service yes <ticket> ...`. `deploy-prod.sh` writes `OMNISIGHT_PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG` when prior tag differs and restarts the unit: `/home/user/sora-bridge/scripts/deploy-prod.sh:275-291`.

- **GAP: G6 is not actually covered by F2 AC.** Coverage map says “F2 guard or follow-up” at draft line 129, but F2’s Code AC lines 64-74 does not include guarding/removing the canary branch. Either add a concrete F2 AC or make an explicit follow-up ticket.

- **OK: dependency graph mostly right.** F1/F2/F3 can be implemented independently because F2 only changes shipped unit/code and F4 is the activation gate. F4 correctly blocks on F1+F2+F3. F4 as the only `tier:X` is right.

Coverage: F1 covers G2, F2 covers G4/G8/G7, F3 covers G3, F4 covers G1/G5. G6 needs explicit handling. The main technical revision is F1’s exact metric/label contract and F3’s backend-b port.
